- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.

# Light Bait
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Fishing
[Light Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 16 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 2x [[Prismarine Crystals]] 1x [[Raw Cod]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LIGHT_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Increases your chance to catch rare Sea Creatures during the day.; COMMON BAIT
Light Bait is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ink Sac II (2) ([[Ink Sac]])|1x [[Raw Cod]] 2x [[Prismarine Crystals]]|Recipe: [[Prismarine Crystals]] x2 + [[Raw Cod]] → Light Bait|
## Usage
### Fishing
Light Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Light Bait will be consumed. During the day it will grant a 25% increase in weight for rare [[Sea Creatures]] (those with a weight below 400). It has no effect at night. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]]|Lily Pad VI (6) ([[Lily Pad]])|1x [[Dark Bait]] [[Light Bait]] 1x Light Bait 1x [[Fish Bait]]|Recipe: [[Fish Bait]] + Light Bait + [[Dark Bait]] → [[Whale Bait]]|
## History

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Locust Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Locust]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion

||
||

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Locust Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Crop Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Crop Speed ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Crop Growth]] while in [[The Garden]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Lonelily
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Lonelily]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LONELILY
Tooltip: A delicate flower that only blooms when left alone, thriving in the stillness of its own space.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; COMMON MUTATION
A Lonelily is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Lonelily can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are no crops within 1 block of [[Dirt]] or farmland.
When placed near another crop, Lonelily allows for additional items to be found.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Lonelilies also drop 525 (8 stacks plus 13) [[Potato]], 613 (9 stacks plus 37) [[Carrot]], 340 (5 stacks plus 20) [[Pumpkin]], and +2,800 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Lonelily can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 50,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 25 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Lonelily grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Lonelillies are used to create a [[Fleshtrap]] and [[Noctilume]].
## Gallery
- Lonelily in the Greenhouse Lonelily in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Trivia
- A Lonelily can grow on [[Dirt]], and will turn it into [[Farmland]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Lonelily.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Lonelily.|
|Feb 2, 2026|Increased Lonelily spawn chance.|

# Magical Milk Bucket
Aliases: Magic Milk Bucket
Categories: Common items, Items
[Magical Milk Bucket]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: See Possible Lores
Source: [[Shania]]
Obtained: Left Clicking [[Shania]] with a bucket
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MAGICAL_MILK_BUCKET
Tooltip: What do you get from a dwarf cow?; Condensed milk.; COMMON
The Magical Milk Bucket is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that allows to be used as a normal [[Milk Bucket]] but never runs out, similar to the [[Magical Water Bucket]] and [[Magical Lava Bucket]]. It can be obtained by milking [[Shania]], the cow found in the [[Farm]].
## Obtaining
Simply take a [[Bucket]], go to the [[Farm]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], and locate [[Shania]] near the [[Farmhand]]. Rapidly click on Shania with a bucket until it turns into a Magical Milk Bucket. This can only be done once per in-game week.
## Possible Lores
The lore of the Magical Milk Bucket will change if the item is dropped, or when switching lobbies.
- What did the mother cow say to the baby cow? It's pasture bed time.
- What do you get when a cow is caught in a earthquake? A MILKSHAKE!!!
- What do you call a cow with a twitch? Beef jerky.
- Where do cows get their medicine? At the farmacy!
- What do you get from a dwarf cow? Condensed milk.
Magical Milk Bucket Magical Milk Bucket Magical Milk Bucket Magical Milk Bucket Magical Milk Bucket
## Trivia
- It used to provide some stat boosts in the first few hours after its release, and gave 100 [[Health]] and 70 [[Defense]] per bucket, which stacked. This was not intentional, and a few hours after its release it was removed.
- This item was not included in the patch notes when it came out, and was first mentioned when they did the patch for the stat boosts.
- When with the effects of a [[God Potion]], the player should be extremely careful not to drink this bucket, as it will remove all effects including positive ones.
## Known Bugs
- Drinking the Magical Milk Bucket can result in [[Potions|night vision]] being removed, and not returning until the player dies.
- For some players, milking [[Shania]] with an empty [[Bucket]] will only give a regular [[Milk Bucket]]. This is a visual client-side glitch, and should not happen unless you right clicked to milk Shania.
- When returning to a [[Private Island|player's island]], the Magical Milk Bucket can return to a regular [[Milk Bucket]] or revert to an empty [[Bucket]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Sep 30, 2026|Improved itemlore of Magical Milk Bucket by adding: Obtained by using a bucket to milk Shania the Cow in The Hub. Can be drank to remove all potion effects and never runs out of milk!.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Magical Milk Bucket.|
|Dec 3, 2020|Stats of this item were removed in a hidden patch.|
Linked from: [[Bucket (disambiguation)]], [[Emerald Blade]], [[Farm]], [[God Potion]], [[God Potion Effect]], [[Magic (disambiguation)]], [[Shania]], [[Vanilla Objects]]

Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from merchants.): 176.5 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MAGICAL_WATER_BUCKET
Tooltip: This Magical Water Bucket will never run out of water, no matter how many times it is emptied.; COMMON
The Magical Water Bucket is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that acts like a normal [[Water Bucket]] but never runs empty, allowing infinite [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] placement. It is unlocked at [[Ice|Ice V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ice V (5) ([[Ice]])|1x [[Enchanted Ice]] 3x [[Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Ice]] → Magical Water Bucket|
## Usage
Upon right-clicking a block on a [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]] while holding a Magical Water Bucket, Water will be placed at the clicked area. Unlike a normal Water Bucket, the Magical Water Bucket will never run empty and so require no refilling, allowing infinite water placement. It's commonly used with the [[Prismapump]] to quickly build farms.
The Magical Water Bucket is also used to unlock the [[Melancholic Viking|Joyful Viking]]'s [[Shops|Shop]] by extinguishing the fire in his cabin (which must be directly right-clicked).
## Trivia
- The Magical Water Bucket was originally suggested by a user called noonoodragon in SkyBlock Ideas - Round 1. noonoodragon received a [[Creative Mind]] for this idea.
- Even though [[Enchanted Ice]], one of the Magical Water Bucket's ingredients, is [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]], the Magical Water Bucket is [[Rarity|COMMON]].
- Its behavior is very similar to the [[Magical Lava Bucket]].
## Demonstration
Magical Water Bucket Demonstration
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Magical Water Bucket.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed the Magical Water Bucket deleting blocks in certain circumstances.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Fixed placing Magical Water Buckets in cauldrons turning into normal buckets.|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Fixed a bug that prevented the Magical Water Bucket from placing Water in non-source blocks.|

The Mine Affinity Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that increases the player's [[Speed]] by +25 on public Mining Islands. It can be bought from the Combat Merchant NPC for 2,500 [[Coins]].
When held in a player's inventory or [[Accessory Bag]], it increases the player's [[Speed]] by +25 in the [[Coal Mine]], [[Gold Mine]], [[Deep Caverns]], [[Dwarven Mines]], and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
The Mine Affinity Talisman can be purchased from the [[Combat Merchant]] for 2,500 [[Coins]].

The Miner Zombie Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Rotten Pickaxe]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Miner Zombie Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Miner Zombie]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Miner Zombie Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Miner Zombie Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Rotten Pickaxe]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Rotten Pickaxe ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3–30 [[Mining Speed]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Miner Zombie Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Miner Zombie Shard has the following description:

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_MINING_SKILL_BOOST_COMMON; PET_ITEM_MINING_SKILL_BOOST_UNCOMMON; PET_ITEM_MINING_SKILL_BOOST_RARE
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Gives +20% pet exp for Mining.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; COMMON PET ITEM
A Mining Exp Boost is a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Obtaining
- A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Mining Exp Boost can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 60,000 [[Coins]].
- An [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Mining Exp Boost can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 250,000 [[Coins]].
- A [[Rarity|RARE]] Mining Exp Boost has a 20% chance to be rewarded for participating in an active [[Dwarven Mines|Mining Event]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]] (i.e. [[Goblin Raid]], [[Mithril Gourmand]], [[Raffle]]).
## Usage
|Rarity|Usage|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Mining Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +20% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Mining]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Mining Exp Boost for 25,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Mining Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +30% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Mining]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Mining Exp Boost for 50,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Mining Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +40% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Mining]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Mining Exp Boost for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Mining Exp Boost.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|RARE ([[Rarity]]) Mining Exp Boost is now a reward from Mining Events ([[Dwarven Mines]]).|

The Mining Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked in the [[Coal]] collection, with upgraded tiers being able to hold a higher capacity of mining-related items. The Beginner Sack can only hold vanilla resources, and the Large Enchanted Mining Sack can hold enchanted versions of items.
## Obtaining
### Merchant
A Beginner Mining Sack can be bought for 250 [[Coins]] at [[Mining Merchant]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] near [[Mining District]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Beginner Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])|Coal II (2) ([[Coal]])|1x [[Leather]] 8x [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] ×8 + [[Leather]] → [[Mining Sack]]|
|Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])|Coal V (5) ([[Coal]])|1x Beginner Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]) 8x [[Enchanted Coal]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Coal]] ×8 + [[Mining Sack]] → [[Mining Sack]]|
|Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])|Coal VII (7) ([[Coal]])|1x Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]) 32x [[Enchanted Coal]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Coal]] x4 ×8 + [[Mining Sack]] → [[Mining Sack]]|
|Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])|Coal IX (9) ([[Coal]])|1x Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]) 64x [[Enchanted Coal]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Coal]] x8 ×8 + [[Mining Sack]] → [[Mining Sack]]|
|Large Enchanted Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])|Coal X (10) ([[Coal]])|12x [[Enchanted Leather]] 16x [[Enchanted String]] 112x (1 stack plus 48 (112 in total)) [[Enchanted Coal]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Coal]] x16 ×7 + [[Enchanted String]] x16 + [[Enchanted Leather]] x12 → [[Mining Sack]]|
## Usage
The Mining Sack can store [[Mining]]-related items.

|-|-|-|

|Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])|2,240|[[Biofuel]], [[Coal]], [[Cobblestone]], [[Diamond]], [[Emerald]], [[End Stone]], [[Flint]], [[Glacite]], [[Glossy Gemstone]], [[Glowstone Dust]], [[Gold Ingot]], [[Gravel]], [[Hard Stone]], [[Ice]], [[Iron Ingot]], [[Lapis Lazuli]], [[Mithril]], [[Mycelium]], [[Nether Quartz]], [[Netherrack]], [[Obsidian]], [[Oil Barrel]], [[Packed Ice]], [[Plasma]], [[Red Sand]], [[Redstone Dust]], [[Refined Mineral]], [[Sand]], [[Snow Block]], [[Starfall]], [[Stone]], [[Sulphur]], [[Sunflower Oil]], [[Titanium]], [[Treasurite]], [[Tungsten]], [[Umber]], [[Volta]]|
|Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])|6,720|(same as above)|

|Large Enchanted Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])|20,160|[[Enchanted Cobblestone]], [[Enchanted Coal]], Enchanted Iron ([[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]), Enchanted Gold ([[Enchanted Gold Ingot]]), [[Enchanted Diamond]], [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]], [[Enchanted Emerald]], Enchanted Redstone ([[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]), [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]], [[Enchanted Obsidian]], [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]], [[Enchanted Sand]], [[Enchanted End Stone]], [[Enchanted Mithril]], [[Enchanted Titanium]], [[Enchanted Hard Stone]], [[Enchanted Red Sand]], [[Enchanted Mycelium]], [[Enchanted Sulphur]], Enchanted Block of Coal ([[Enchanted Coal Block]]), [[Enchanted Diamond Block]], [[Enchanted Emerald Block]], [[Enchanted Glowstone]], [[Enchanted Gold Block]], [[Enchanted Iron Block]], Enchanted Lapis Block ([[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]]), [[Enchanted Mycelium Cube]], [[Enchanted Quartz Block]], [[Enchanted Red Sand Cube]], [[Enchanted Redstone Block]], [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]], [[Enchanted Titanium]], [[Enchanted Glacite]], [[Enchanted Tungsten]], [[Enchanted Umber]]|

Museum: ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 40,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials: 42,304x [[Oak Log]] 2x [[Minion Storage X-pender]]
Bazaar Material cost: (not including: 2 [[Minion Storage X-pender]])
Raw Materials to upgrade: 1x [[Minion Storage X-pender]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: XXLARGE_ENCHANTED_CHEST
Tooltip: Place this chest next to a minion and the minion will store items inside once its storage is full!; Storage space: 3 items; COMMON
Minion Storages are a type of chest for [[Minions]], used when they run out of inventory space. They are not [[Minion Upgrades]], but are instead placed next to a minion.
## Usage
Minion Storages can be placed on any side of a Minion (but not under and above the minion), which will increase the effective storage capacity of the Minion based on the type of Minion Storage used.
|Minion Storage type|Added Storage|
|-|-|
|Small|3 slots|
|Medium|9 slots|
|Large|15 slots|
|X-Large|21 slots|
|XX-Large|27 slots|
If [[Automated Shipping]] is used, it will only sell after both the Minion's inventory and chest are filled.
Note that [[Hopper|Hoppers]] do not work on Minion Storages as the items are not stored in the vanilla chest representing the Minion Storage.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Recipe|Collection ([[Collections]])|Cost to Upgrade (Bazaar/Bits Shop)|Cost to Craft (Bazaar/Bits Shop)|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Minion Storage]]; Small Storage|Recipe: [[Oak Log]] x8 ×8 → [[Minion Storage]]|Oak Log IV (4) ([[Oak Log]])|||
|[[Minion Storage]]; Medium Storage|Recipe: [[Enchanted Oak Log]] ×8 + [[Minion Storage]] → [[Minion Storage]]|Oak Log VI (6) ([[Oak Log]])|||
|[[Minion Storage]]; Large Storage|Recipe: [[Enchanted Oak Log]] x32 ×8 + [[Minion Storage]] → [[Minion Storage]]|Oak Log IX (9) ([[Oak Log]])|||
|[[Minion Storage]]; X-Large Storage|Recipe: [[Minion Storage X-pender]] + [[Minion Storage]] → [[Minion Storage]]||1,500 [[Bits]]|1,500 [[Bits]]|
|[[Minion Storage]]; XX-Large Storage|Recipe: [[Minion Storage X-pender]] + [[Minion Storage]] → [[Minion Storage]]||1,500 [[Bits]]|3,000 [[Bits]]|
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Placing a Minion Storage not next to a minion|You need to place this next to a minion!|
|Placing a Minion Storage next to a normal chest|You can't place minion storage next to another chest!|
|Placing a Minion Storage next to a trapped chest|You can't place this next to other chests!|
|Placing a Minion Storage next to another Minion Storage|You can't place a chest here!|
|Placing a Minion Storage next to minions that all already have Minion Storages|This minion already has a chest!|
## Tips
- Medium storages are used the most often because they are cheap and provide decent storage space.
- It is best used with compactors.
## Bugs
- Items can be put into the vanilla chest representing the Minion Storage with a dropper and taken out with a hopper.
  - This does not put items in the minion's storage, but in the vanilla chest that is used to represent the Minion Storage.
  - A hopper cannot put items into the representing chest.
  - Items put into the representing chest are saved and will still be there after reloading the island.
  - Breaking the Minion Storage or picking up the accompanying minion will make any items in the representing chest drop on the ground.
## Trivia
- The game messages about not being able to place Minion Storages next to other chests take priority over needing to place it next to a minion.
- Placing a Large Storage chest grants the achievement [[Achievements|Bigger Storage is Seeded]].
- A newly placed minion storage links to the first minion it finds while checking the cardinal directions in the following order: south, west, north, east (from the perspective of the chest).
## Gallery
- A Cobblestone Minion with a Minion Storage beside it.
## History

|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Small, Medium and Large Minion Chests.|
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added X-Large and XX-Large Minion Chests.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Minion Chests to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Changed the collection requirement for Medium Storage from Oak Log VII (7) ([[Oak Log]]) to Oak Log VI (6) ([[Oak Log]]).|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MINNOW_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +25 Fishing Speed.; COMMON BAIT
Minnow Bait is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
Minnow Bait can be bought from the [[Fishing Merchant|Fish Merchant]] for 15 [[Coins]]. (Up to 640 per day)
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Salmon I (1) ([[Raw Salmon]])|2x [[Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Raw Cod]] ×2 → Minnow Bait|
### Seal's Treat Bag
128x Minnow Bait have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Minnow Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Minnow Bait will be consumed to grant +25 [[Fishing Speed]]. Note that the [[Fishing Speed]] doesn't show up in the player's stats. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.

## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Mist Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|COMMON]] and Water Category
- Second: [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] or higher

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Mist Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Fog Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Fog Elemental ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Intelligence]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Mithril is a [[Dwarven Metals|Dwarven Metal]] in the [[Mining]] Collection that can be obtained in the [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]] and [[Glacite Tunnels]] by mining Mithril veins, while having at least 4 [[Breaking Power]].
## Collection
Collecting Mithril increases the player's Mithril [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of Mithril.

## Usage
### [[Titanium]]
When mining any Mithril Ore within the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Glacite Tunnels]], there is a 2% base chance that [[Titanium]] Ore will spawn in its place. This chance is increased by the Titanium Insanium [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk, up to 7%.
[[Titanium]] does not spawn upon mining Mithril within the [[Crystal Hollows]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Mithril Minion I ([[Mithril Minion]])|Mithril I (1)|1x [[Wooden Pickaxe]] [[Mithril]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Mithril|Recipe: Mithril x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Pickaxe]] → [[Mithril Minion]]|
|Mithril Minion II ([[Mithril Minion]])|Mithril I (1)|1x Mithril Minion I ([[Mithril Minion]]) [[Mithril]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Mithril|Recipe: Mithril x20 ×8 + [[Mithril Minion]] → [[Mithril Minion]]|
|Mithril Minion III ([[Mithril Minion]])|Mithril I (1)|1x Mithril Minion II ([[Mithril Minion]]) [[Mithril]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Mithril|Recipe: Mithril x40 ×8 + [[Mithril Minion]] → [[Mithril Minion]]|
|Mithril Minion IV ([[Mithril Minion]])|Mithril I (1)|1x Mithril Minion III ([[Mithril Minion]]) [[Mithril]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Mithril|Recipe: Mithril x64 ×8 + [[Mithril Minion]] → [[Mithril Minion]]|
|[[Enchanted Mithril]]|Diamond III (3) ([[Diamond]])|[[Mithril]] 160x Mithril|Recipe: Mithril x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Mithril]]|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Mithril Golem Pet]]|Mithril III (3)|1x [[Enchanted Egg]] [[Mithril]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Mithril|Recipe: Mithril x32 ×8 + [[Enchanted Egg]] → [[Mithril Golem Pet]]|
### [[The Forge|Forging]] Ingredient
|Slot|Name|Forging ([[The Forge]]) recipe|
|-|-|-|
|[[Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Forge]]|[[Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Forge]]|1 [[Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Forge]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Titanium]] 16 [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Ender Pearl]] [[Mithril]] 48 Mithril 25,000 (390 stacks plus 40) [[Coins]]|
### [[Potions|Brewing]] Ingredient
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Spelunker I Potion ([[Spelunker Potion]])|Mithril II (2)|[[Mithril]] 1x Mithril 3x [[Water Bottle]]|Recipe: Mithril → [[Spelunker Potion]], [[Spelunker Potion]], [[Spelunker Potion]]|
## Trivia
- Mithril is a fictional material originating from J.R.R Tolkien's Middle Earth universe. Dwarves forged it into armor that cannot be pierced by even the strongest blades. This is referenced not only shown by the name of the Dwarven Metal but also by the [[Mithril Coat]], a direct reference to Tolkien's Middle-earth Mithril.
  - This material also appears in Terraria, albeit spelled Mythril, rather than Mithril.
- Inside the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Glacite Tunnels]], only the Mithril in the texture of Gray Wool and Cyan Hardened Clay can be insta-mined. Light Blue Wool Mithril cannot be insta-mined, even inside the [[Crystal Hollows]].

### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Moonflower]]|Moonflower I (1)|[[Moonflower]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Moonflower|Recipe: Moonflower x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Moonflower]]|
## Gallery
- Moonflower in the Greenhouse Moonflower in the [[Greenhouse]]
### Growth Stages of Moonflower in the Garden
- Stage 1
- Stage 2
- Stage 3
- Stage 4
## Tips
- In order to obtain the first Moonflower to plant on an [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] or [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] profile, players must get a [[Dragonfly]] [[Pests|Pest]], vacuum it up, and then paste the default Sunflower preset in the [[The Garden|Desk]] using the [[Enchanted Sunflower|Enchanted Sunflowers]]. After that, they can harvest the pasted crops at nighttime to get regular Moonflowers. Harvesting them at daytime will grant [[Sunflower|Sunflowers]] instead.
  - Alternatively, players may obtain it by growing a [[Godseed]] in the [[Greenhouse]]. However, this is not recommended because it takes a really long time. In addition, Greenhouse crop drops are automatically compacted, so it may still be necessary to paste the default preset using the drops.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Moonflower.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Moonflower.|
|Oct 15, 2026|Fixed Moonflowers in [[Greenhouse]] not providing Farming Tool EXP ([[Farming Tools]]).|

# Move Jerry
Aliases: Jerry Egg
Categories: Common items, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Move Jerry]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ASSISTANT
Tooltip: Place this where you would like Jerry to move to!; COMMON
Move Jerry is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
A Move Jerry can be claimed from [[Jerry]] after clicking [[Jerry|Move Jerry]] in their [[Jerry|interface]]. If the player already has a Move Jerry in their inventory, [[Jerry]] will not give the player another one. This can be bypassed by storing the Move Jerry in a different inventory and claiming another one.
## Usage
### Moving Jerry
A Move Jerry can be used to change [[Jerry]]'s location on the player's [[Private Island]]. The new spawn location has to be on top of a solid block, and cannot be placed above the void.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Aspect of the Jerry]]|None|1x [[Stick]] [[Move Jerry]] 2x Move Jerry|Recipe: Move Jerry ×2 + [[Stick]] → [[Aspect of the Jerry]]|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Jerry Pet]]|None|1x [[Egg]] [[Move Jerry]] 8x Move Jerry|Recipe: Move Jerry ×8 + [[Egg]] → [[Jerry Pet]]|
### Jerry Pet Upgrade
Upgrading [[Jerry Pet]] at [[Kat]] requires Move Jerry.
|Rarity|Ingredients|
|-|-|
|C ([[Rarity]]) → U ([[Rarity]])|[[Move Jerry]] 1x Move Jerry|
|U ([[Rarity]]) → R ([[Rarity]])|[[Move Jerry]] 1x Move Jerry|
|R ([[Rarity]]) → E ([[Rarity]])|[[Move Jerry]] 1x Move Jerry|
|E ([[Rarity]]) → L ([[Rarity]])|[[Move Jerry]] 1x Move Jerry|
It takes [[Move Jerry]] 4 Move Jerry in total to upgrade a [[Jerry Pet]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
### Mutations
In order to obtain a [[Jerryflower]], the [[Fertilized Jerryseed]] needs to be given [[Move Jerry]] 10 Move Jerry to continue growing past Growth Stage 5.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Claiming|Use the egg I gave you to click and point to where you want me to go!|
|Placing Above Void|Why would you try? :(|
|Placing on Public Island or [[The Garden]]|You can't move Jerry here! He doesn't belong here!|
|Placing While Guesting|Guests can't place Jerry or Sam! But you know that, right?|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Added Move Jerry.|
|Jul 15, 2019|Fixed being able to place Move Jerrys in water on public islands.|

The Mudworm Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Visitor Bait]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Floor Drops
A Mudworm Shard can be obtained from [[Floor Drops]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]]. It has a 33.02% (1 in 3.03 rolls) chance to drop from one.
### Huntraps
A Mudworm Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Moonglade Marsh]] (On Land).
### Fusion
A Mudworm Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Mudworm Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Visitor Bait]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Visitor Bait ([[Attributes]]); Garden Visitors ([[The Garden]]) arrive +1%–10% faster.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Mudworm Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Mudworm Shard has the following description:
- A silent tiller revered for its unseen but vital role in the life cycle of the earth.

Tooltip: The life cycle of a mushroom starts with the spore, which germs and becomes Mycelium, which develops fruit bodies into a mature mushroom, and that mushroom will release spores.; COMMON
The Mushroom Spore is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Mushroom Spores can be obtained from breaking Brown Mushrooms and Brown Mushroom Blocks in the [[Mystic Marsh]] or [[Scarleton]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. Both the Mushrooms and Mushroom Blocks require the player to use a [[Spore Harvester]], which can be bought from the [[Mage Emissary]], to break them.
## Usage
### [[Charminizer]]
[[Mushroom Spore]] 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) Mushroom Spores, along with 100,000 [[Coins]] can be traded to the [[Mage Emissary]] for a [[Charminizer]].
### [[Marsh Spore Soup]]
[[Mushroom Spore]] 5 Mushroom Spores, along with 950 [[Coins]] can be traded to [[Ezekiel]] for a [[Marsh Spore Soup]].
### [[Scarleton Premium]]
[[Mushroom Spore]] 50 Mushroom Spores, along with 19,000 [[Coins]] can be traded to [[Ezekiel]] for a [[Scarleton Premium]].
## Trivia
- Mushroom Spores are affected by [[Farming Fortune]], unlike [[Red Thornleaf]].

Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 15x [[Nether Wart]] 30x [[Soul Sand]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 30 Soul Sand)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NETHER_WART_ISLAND
Tooltip: Place this in your private world to spawn a Nether Wart island!; COMMON
The Nether Wart Island is a [[Spawnable Islands|Spawnable Island]] unlocked at [[Netherrack|Netherrack I (1)]].
Placing it creates an Island composed of [[Soul Sand]], [[Obsidian]], [[Nether Wart]], [[Nether Brick|Nether Bricks]], [[Nether Brick Slab|Nether Brick Slabs]], and [[Nether Brick Stairs]].
It also contains a [[Floating Crystals|Nether Wart Crystal]], which grows [[Nether Wart]] on [[Soul Sand]], with an effective radius of 5.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Netherrack I (1) ([[Netherrack]])|15x [[Nether Wart]] 30x [[Soul Sand]]|Recipe: [[Nether Wart]] x5 ×3 + [[Soul Sand]] x5 ×6 → Nether Wart Island|
## Island Contents
A Nether Wart Island contains the following:
- 1,178 (18 stacks plus 26) [[Soul Sand]]
- 478 (7 stacks plus 30) [[Obsidian]]
- 75 (1 stack plus 11) [[Nether Brick]]
- 24 [[Nether Brick Stairs]]
- 14 [[Nether Brick Slab]]
- 12 [[Nether Wart]]

Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 640x (10 stacks (640 items total)) Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 640x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NIGHT_VISION_CHARM
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Grants Night Vision while this item is in your inventory.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
The Night Vision Charm is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Mushroom|Mushroom VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Mushroom VII (7) ([[Mushroom]])|4x [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] 4x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] ×4 → Night Vision Charm|
## Usage
The Night Vision Charm gives permanent [[Night Vision Potion|Night Vision]] I.
## Trivia
- Before the 0.7.9 Wardrobe Update, when a player died with the Night Vision Charm in their inventory or Accessory Bag, the Night Vision effect would stay deactivated unless the player reapplied the accessory.
- Other items providing Night Vision such as [[Mushroom Armor]] and the [[Bat Pet]] overwrite this effect, causing Night Vision to be lost when their effects disappear.
- The Night Vision Charm grants the player [[Night Vision Potion|Night Vision II]], although this has exactly the same effect as [[Night Vision Potion|Night Vision I]].

# Pandora's Box
Aliases: Pandora
Categories: Common items, Accessories, Pages with UIs
[Pandora's Box]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PANDORAS_BOX
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Complete challenges to earn stats and level up the Pandora's Box.; Challenges completed: 0\ 20; You receive +1  and +1  per 2 challenges you complete.; Note! You must have this item in your inventory or accessory bag when completing a challenge!; Right-click to open!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
Pandora's Box is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] obtained from [[Shen's Auction]].
## Obtaining
### Shen's Auction
The Pandora's Box appears in [[Shen's Auction]], and can be won if the player is one of the top 20 bidders on the item when the auction ends. [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles have a separate auction, where top 10 bidders get the item. When [[Diaz|Mayor Diaz]] is elected with the Volume trading perk, the quantity is doubled to 40 top bids (20 for [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]]).
### Raffle of the Century
A Pandora's Box can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Pandora's Box unlocks 20 Challenges, which can be completed while it is in the player's inventory or [[Accessory Bag]]. For every 2 challenges completed the player gets +1 [[Health]] and +1 [[Strength]], up to +10 of each. Its rarity is not upgraded when [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]], and can only be increased by completing challenges.
### Rarity
|Completed Challenges|Rarity|
|-|-|
|0|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|3|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|5|RARE ([[Rarity]])|
|7|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|
|9|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|
|20|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|
### List Of Challenges
|Rarity of Challenges|Name|Requirement|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Intelligence Seeker|Reach 5555 Intelligence outside of dungeons.|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Ghost Combo|Reach a combo of 75 Ghosts.|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Nuclei Claimer|Complete the Crystal Nucleus 2 times within 5 minutes.|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Farming Prodigy|Place in the top 1% during Jacob's Farming Contest.|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Dragon Puncher|Place 1st in damage on an Ender Dragon without using any ranged damage.|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Mastery Master|Get exactly 320 points from shooting (10) Red Targets during a Test of Mastery at Dojo.|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Crypt Exploder|Blow up 75 Crypts in a single dungeon run.|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Trophy Fisher|Fish up 8 different trophy fish in under 10 minutes.|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Ashfang Destroyer|Deal at least 98% of damage to an Ashfang.|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Pristine Proc'er|Achieve at least a 150x Flawed Gemstone Pristine find.|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Voidgloom Slasher|Kill a Voidgloom Seraph Tier IV in under 40 seconds.|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Dungeoneer|Obtain an S+ score in Floor 7 of the Catacombs without using any teleportation ability.|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Revenant Slasher|Solo kill a Revenant Horror Tier V without wearing any armor pieces (must be done outside of the Crystal Hollows).|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Jawbus Destroyer|Deal at least 98% of damage to a Lord Jawbus.|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|M7 Tanks|Beat Master Mode Floor 7 of the Catacombs with 5 Tanks.|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|M7 Healers|Beat Master Mode Floor 7 of the Catacombs with 5 Healers.|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|M7 Berserkers|Beat Master Mode Floor 7 of the Catacombs with 5 Berserkers.|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|M7 Archers|Beat Master Mode Floor 7 of the Catacombs with 5 Archers.|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|M7 Mages|Beat Master Mode Floor 7 of the Catacombs with 5 Mages.|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|Damage Exacter|Deal exactly 333,333 damage.|
## Notes
- If a Pandora's Box is traded to another player, it will keep its rarity. However, if they open it, it will gain or lose rarity based on the number of challenges they've done.
- The M7 challenges can be completed even if players leave, as long as there were 5 players with the respective class at the beginning of the dungeon.
- The Dragon Puncher challenge cannot be done with [[Halberd of the Shredded]], but can be done with [[Flaming Flay]].
- The Dungeoneer challenge considers the following as a teleportation ability: [[Spirit Leap]], [[Hyperion]]'s Wither Impact ability, [[Ender Pearl]], and [[Aspect of the End]]'s Instant Transmission. However, it does not count [[Hyperion]]'s Wither Impact ability as a teleportation ability during the Floor 7 boss fight.

Park Armor is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Armor|Armor Set]] composed of [[Mole Hat]], [[Kelly's T-Shirt]], [[Charlie's Trousers]], and [[Melody's Shoes]].
## Obtaining
Park Armor is obtained by completing quests on [[The Park]].
- [[Charlie's Trousers]] are a reward from [[Charlie]] for completing the "Into the Woods" quest.
- [[Kelly's T-Shirt]] is a reward from [[Kelly]] for completing the "A Helping Hand" quest.
- [[Melody's Shoes]] are a reward from [[Melody]] for completing the "The Rebuild" quest.
- [[Mole Hat]] is a reward from [[Molbert]] for completing the "Mole Problems" quest.

# Parkour Checkpoint
Categories: Common items, Blocks, Social
[Parkour Checkpoint]
Type: Block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Skill Level ([[Skills]]) Requirement: Social I (1) ([[Social]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Amelia]]
Buy: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PARKOUR_POINT
Tooltip: Create checkpoints for parkour courses on your private island!; ❣ Requires Social Skill 1.; COMMON
Parkour Checkpoint is a block that can be placed on the [[Private Island]]. It can be bought from [[Amelia]] for 1,000 [[Coins]], and requires [[Social|Social I (1)]] to purchase or place.
## Usage
- It is used along with [[Parkour Start/End]] to create parkour courses.
- When right-clicking on the block, a menu appears that can be used to change its properties. Set the name to that of the parkour course it is associated with, and set the number to where it is in the course.
## Trivia
- Checkpoints cannot be set if the parkour has not been started, or have gone out of order.
- If the player falls into the void while the parkour is active, they will not be teleported back to the checkpoint but instead be teleported to the spawn point or Hub Portal on your Island.
- A player must walk over every checkpoint to finish the parkour
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 12, 2020|0.9.12|Added Parkour Checkpoint.|
Linked from: [[Changelog/2020/November 12]], [[Parkour Start/End]], [[Social]], [[Social/Leveling Rewards]]

# Parkour Start/End
Aliases: Parkour End, Parkour Start, Parkour Start End
Categories: Common items, Blocks, Pages with UIs, Social

Type: Block

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Amelia]]
Buy: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PARKOUR_CONTROLLER
Tooltip: Create the start\ end of parkour courses on your private island!; ❣ Requires Social Skill 1.; COMMON
The Parkour Start/End is a block that can be bought from [[Amelia]] for 1,000 [[Coins]]. It is used to set the start/end of a parkour course on a [[Private Island]]. 2 are bought from [[Amelia]] at once.
## Usage
The Parkour Start/End is used to start or end a parkour course on a [[Private Island]]. It gives island points to those who complete it. To make a complete parkour course, the player must purchase at least two [[Parkour Start/End]] Parkour Start/Ends, one to start the parkour , and one to end it. To create checkpoints, the player must use [[Parkour Checkpoint|Parkour Checkpoints]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 12, 2020|0.9.12|Added Parkour Start/End.|

The Personal Bank Item is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item used to access the [[Bank]] remotely. The cooldown of the item is the same as the cooldown of the player's personal bank.
## Usage
Right-clicking with the Personal Bank Item will open the [[Bank|Banking]] interface, whilst left-clicking will deposit all [[Coins]] in the player's purse.

### Crafting

Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!

The Pet Cake is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that gives 10,000 Pet XP.
## Obtaining
Pet Cakes can be obtained by completing a visitor request for [[Oringo]] in [[The Garden]].
## Usage
Right-Clicking with a Pet Cake in hand will add 10,000 Pet XP to the player's active Pet. Pet Cakes don't have any limits, unlike [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candy]].

The Phanflare Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Moonglade Mastery]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Phanflare Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Phanflare]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]].
### Huntraps
A Phanflare Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Moonglade Marsh]] (On Land).
### Fusion
A Phanflare Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Phanflare Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Moonglade Mastery]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Moonglade Mastery ([[Attributes]]); Grants +10–100 [[Fig Fortune]] and +10–100 [[Mangrove Fortune]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Pocket Espresso Machine
Categories: Common items, Accessories, Slayer, Enderman Slayer, Mob Drops
[Pocket Espresso Machine]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Drop Chance: 9Pray RNGesus ([[Drop Chance]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Enderman Slayer IV (4) ([[Enderman Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POCKET_ESPRESSO_MACHINE
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Costs 1⸎ Soulflow per 5s in combat:; ⁍ Gain +100 Intelligence; Enabled: On Right-click to toggle!; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 4.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
Pocket Espresso Machine is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It increases [[Intelligence]] at a [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] cost.
## Obtaining
The Pocket Espresso Machine is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from [[Voidgloom Seraph|Tier IV Voidgloom Seraph]] if the player has [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL IV (4)]] or higher. It has a 0.39% (1 in 256.16) chance to drop.
## Usage
Pocket Espresso Machine requires [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL IV (4)]] to use.
Accessory:
Pocket Espresso Machine
[Effects]
[Costs 1 Soulflow (Soulflow (Mechanic)) per 5s in combat: Gain 100 Intelligence]
Its ability can be toggled on and off by right clicking with it.
When in combat with it enabled and sufficient [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]], it gives the player +100 [[Intelligence]] and takes away 1 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] every 5 seconds.
### Salvaging
Pocket Espresso Machine can be salvaged for 15 [[Dragon Essence]].
## Trivia
- Due to its rarity and price, it is commonly referred to as Depresso within the playerbase.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Added Pocket Espresso Machine.|
|Aug 17, 2021|Fixed the item ability working regardless of if the user has the required Slayer level.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Pocket Espresso Machine can now be salvaged for 15 [[Dragon Essence]].|
|Feb 22, 2024|Fixed item requirements only affecting whether or not Pocket Espresso Machine can be obtained as a drop.|

# Pond Island
Categories: Common items, Items, Craftable items, Raw Cod Collection, Spawnable islands, Blocks, Fishing
[Pond Island]
Type: Island Spawner
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Raw Cod IV (4) ([[Raw Cod]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 15x [[Sand]] 60x [[Prismarine Crystals]] 9x [[Iron Ingot]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POND_ISLAND
Tooltip: Place this in your private world to spawn a Pond island!; COMMON
The Pond Island is a [[Spawnable Islands|Spawnable Island]] unlocked at [[Raw Cod|Raw Cod IV (4)]].
Placing it creates an Island composed of [[Sand]], [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]], [[Prismarine]], [[Prismarine Bricks]], [[Dark Prismarine]], and [[Stone]].
It also contains a [[Floating Crystals|Fishing Crystal]], which grants a chance of fishing [[Prismarine Shard|Prismarine Shards]] and [[Prismarine Crystals|Crystals]] in nearby water, with an effective radius of 15.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Cod IV (4) ([[Raw Cod]])|3x [[Water Bucket]] 15x [[Sand]] 60x [[Prismarine Crystals]]|Recipe: [[Water Bucket]] ×3 + [[Sand]] x5 ×3 + [[Prismarine Crystals]] x20 ×3 → Pond Island|
## Island Contents
A Pond Island contains the following:
- 927 (14 stacks plus 31) [[Stone]]
- 368 (5 stacks plus 48) [[Prismarine]]
- 180 (2 stacks plus 52) [[Sand]]
- 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]]
- 69 (1 stack plus 5) [[Prismarine Bricks]]
- 27 [[Dark Prismarine]]
- 6 [[Long Grass]]
## Trivia
- This item used to be used for unlocking the Sand collection along with the [[Desert Island]]. However, as of update 0.11.4, sand can now be mined in the [[Mushroom Desert]].
- Unlike when crafting a [[Cake]], crafting a Pond Island consumes the [[Bucket|Buckets]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Pond Island.|

# Prismarine Sinker
Categories: Common items, Items, Craftable items, Fishing, Tools
[Prismarine Sinker]
Type: Rod Part ([[Rod Parts]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Skill Level ([[Skills]]) Requirement: Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PRISMARINE_SINKER
Tooltip: Materializes Prismarine Shard or Prismarine Crystals in your inventory whenever you catch something.; Talk to Roddy in the Backwater Bayou to apply this Sinker to a Fishing Rod.; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 5.; COMMON ROD PART
A Prismarine Sinker is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Rod Parts|Rod Part]] that grants a [[Prismarine Shard]] or [[Prismarine Crystals]] when catching a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] or item.
## Obtaining
The Prismarine Sinker is unlocked at [[Prismarine Shard|Prismarine Shard IV (4)]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8x [[Prismarine Crystals]] 8x [[Prismarine Shard]] 16x [[Lily Pad]]|Recipe: [[Lily Pad]] x16 + [[Prismarine Crystals]] x8 + [[Prismarine Shard]] x8 → Prismarine Sinker|
## Usage
Applying a Prismarine Sinker at [[Roddy]] grants a [[Prismarine Shard]] or [[Prismarine Crystals]] when catching a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] or item.
## Tips
- A Prismarine Sinker can be useful for [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] players trying to sustain [[Whale Bait]]. It is better than using a [[Stingy Sinker]] assuming [[Prismarine Crystals]] are the bottleneck.

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✕ No
Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: RABBIT_MAFIOSO
Tooltip: Hitman Cooldown I The cooldown of the Rabbit Hitman is reduced by +2.5%.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; COMMON FOREST SHARD (ID C22)
The Rabbit Mafioso Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Hitman Cooldown]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Rabbit Mafioso Shard can be purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]] for 1,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]] after reaching [[Chocolate Factory]] level 2. The player can purchase up to 24 each [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]].
### Fusion
A Rabbit Mafioso Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Rabbit Mafioso Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Hitman Cooldown]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Hitman Cooldown ([[Attributes]]); The cooldown of the Rabbit Hitman is reduced by +2.5%–25%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Rabbit Mafioso Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Rabbit Mafioso Shard has the following description:
- Gotta see off the competition somehow.

# Red Thornleaf
Categories: Common items, Items, Crimson Isle
[Red Thornleaf]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RED_THORNLEAF
Tooltip: A leaf that grows on the ground. Really pushing the envelope.; UNCOMMON
Red Thornleaf is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Red Thornleaves can be obtained from breaking [[Dead Bush|Dead Bushes]] in the [[Burning Desert]] or [[Dragontail]] with a [[Thornleaf Scythe]].
## Usage
### Purchase
[[Red Thornleaf Tea]] can be purchased from [[Grog]] in [[Dragontail]].
|Merchant|Price|
|-|-|
|[[Grog]]|[[Red Thornleaf]] 50x Red Thornleaf 20,000 [[Coins]]|
Red Thornleaf can be also used as [[Machine Fuel]], adding 200 Fuel.
## Trivia
- Due to being a [[Dead Bush]], the block version of the Red Thornleaf can be right-clicked with [[Enchanted Bone Meal]] to grant 1 [[Dead Seed]] whilst the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is in the [[Village]].
- Red Thornleaf is not affected by [[Farming Fortune]], unlike [[Mushroom Spore|Mushroom Spores]].

Romero and Juliette Quest Items are quest items obtained from [[Romero and Juliette]] quest. Players have to obtain them from [[Romero]] by bringing him the correct items, and then bring the quest items to [[Juliette]] for rewards.
## Quest Items from Romero
|Quest Step|Item Name|Items Required by Romero to get that item|Reward from Juliette for bringing that item|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Poorly Wrapped Rock|1 Yellow Rock ([[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]])|Shiny Yellow Rock ([[Ring of Love]])|
|2|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Rose Bouquet|15 Poppy ([[Flowers]])|Yellow Rock of Love ([[Ring of Love]])|
|3|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Fake Emerald Altar|64 (1 stack) [[Emerald]]|Mediocre Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]])|
|4|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Mushroom & Warts Stew|1 [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]]|Rubbish Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]])|
|5|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Chunk of the Moon|1x Rabbit VI Potion ([[Rabbit Potion]]) with a [[Cheap Coffee]] or [[Decent Coffee]] base|Modest Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]])|
|6|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Secret Gift for Juliette|1 [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]]|Refined Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]])|
|7|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Solved Rubix Prism|1291 [[Intelligence]] (not [[Mana]])|Classy Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]])|
|8|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Poem of True Love|1 [[Emerald Blade]]|Exquisite Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]])|
|9|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Flower Maelstrom|1 [[Flower Minion]]|Invaluable Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]])|
|10|[[Romero and Juliette Quest Items]] Very Official Yellow Rock of Love!|Wearing a [[Tuxedo]] from [[Taylor]] (will not be consumed)|Legendary Ring of Love ([[Ring of Love]])|

## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|I|25|[[Travel Scroll to Torrhus Springs]] Recipe|+4|

|VIII|10,000|[[Large Pocket Black Hole]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|25,000|[[Veilshroom Bracelet]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Harvesting
Ruby Veilshrooms can be harvested in caves found in the [[Torrhus Springs]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. When collected, each Ruby Veilshroom drops 4. They are unaffected by [[Foraging Fortune]]; they are, however, affected by the Collector perk from [[Heart of the Forest]]. Additionally, it respawns after 50-55 seconds.

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Salmon I (1) ([[Raw Salmon]])|8x [[Raw Salmon]]|Recipe: [[Raw Salmon]] ×8 → Salmon Hat|
### Raffle of the Century
During the first [[Raffle of the Century]], there were 5,000x Salmon Hats available in the [[Raffle of the Century|Speed Raffle]], 15,000x available in the [[Raffle of the Century|Daily Raffle]] and 60,000x available in [[Raffle of the Century|The Big One]].
During future [[Raffle of the Century]] events, a Salmon Hat could be obtained as a minor reward, or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
Salmon Hats obtained from [[Raffle of the Century]] have [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration V]] by default.
## Usage
Helmet:
Salmon Hat [[Strength]]: +15 [[Speed]]: +2
[Effects]
[Ability: Water Burst SNEAK; When wearing the full set, grants the wearer with the ability to burst forward when sneaking in water.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 20]
## Trivia
- Salmon Hat was originally an item marked as [Coming Soon] (This item or feature is 'Coming Soon', which means it is not yet released.). Through bugs, however, it could be crafted for a short time.
  - Salmon Hats crafted this way have [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration I]] instead of the intended [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration IV]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].

Sam's Scythe is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Hoes|Hoe]].
## Obtaining
Sam's Scythe is given to the player by [[Sam]] after purchasing their first [[The Garden|Garden]] plot.
Sam's Scythe can also be repurchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 2,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Upon usage, Sam's Scythe will automatically clear blocks in a 3x3 area. It will clear [[Leaves]], [[Flowers]], [[Grass]] and [[Fern]].
## Tips
- [[Shears]] with [[Efficiency|Efficiency V]] can clear leaves faster than Sam's Scythe.
## Trivia
- Despite having "Scythe" in its name, it is not classified as one.
- Upgrading this item to the [[Garden Scythe]] via SkyMart makes it no longer [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]].

The Scavenger Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that causes all monsters to drop extra [[Coins]] when killed.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Scavenger Talisman can be purchased from the [[Combat Merchant]] for 10,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Scavenger Talisman
[Effects]
[Grants 0.5 Coins per level of the killed enemy.]
Monsters drop 0.5 [[Coins]] per level, in addition to any coins dropped without it. Thus, the accessory will make up its cost after slaying 20,000 levels worth of monsters with it.
This effect stacks with the [[Scavenger]] enchantment, allowing a player to receive 2.3 [[Coins]] per level with [[Scavenger|Scavenger VI]] and the Accessory.
### Crafting

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Scavenger Ring]]|None|[[Scavenger Talisman]] 1x Scavenger Talisman 4x [[Blood-Stained Coins]] 8x [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]|Recipe: [[Blood-Stained Coins]] ×4 + [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] x2 ×4 + Scavenger Talisman → [[Scavenger Ring]]|

# Scourroot
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Scourroot]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SCOURROOT
Tooltip: Bitter to the core, as if cursed by the soil.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; COMMON MUTATION
A Scourroot is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Scourroot can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 1 [[Potato]] and 1 [[Carrot]] within 1 block of farmland.
When placed near another crop, Scourroot increases [[Farming]] Experience by 20% and grants immunity to negative effects.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Scourroots also drop 102 (1 stack plus 38) [[Potato]], 119 (1 stack plus 55) [[Carrot]] and +272 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Scourroot can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 10,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 5 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Scourroot grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Scourroots are used to create a [[Coalroot]] and [[Do-not-eat-shroom]].
## Gallery
- Scourroot in the Greenhouse Scourroot in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Scourroot.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Scourroot.|
|Feb 2, 2026|Reduced Scourroot growing requirements to 1 [[Potato]] and 1 [[Carrot]].|

## Collection

## Obtaining
### Harvesting
Sea Lumies can be obtained in groups of 1 to 4 by breaking sea pickles in the [[Moonglade Marsh]] specifically in [[Murkwater Depths]]. The [[Heart of the Forest]] perk [[Heart of the Forest|Collector]] and a [[Attributes|Yummy]] attribute both give a 2–100% chance to drop double Sea Lumies.

# Shadevine
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Shadevine]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHADEVINE
Tooltip: Tough and tangled, it grows where few others can.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; COMMON MUTATION
A Shadevine is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Shadevine can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 1 [[Cactus]] and 1 [[Sugar Cane]] within 1 block of [[Sand]] or farmland.
When placed near another crop, Shadevine increases [[Farming]] experience by 30%, retains watering status by 100%, and decreases yield by 20%.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Shadevines also drop 78 (1 stack plus 14) [[Cactus]], 104 (1 stack plus 40) [[Sugar Cane]], and +416 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Shadevine can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 10,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 5 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Shadevine grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Shadevines are used to create a [[Duskbloom]].
## Gallery
- Shadevine in the Greenhouse Shadevine in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Shadevine.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Shadevine.|
|Feb 2, 2026|Reduced Shadevine growing requirements to 1 [[Cactus]] and 1 [[Sugar Cane]].|

The Shyworm Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Subterranean Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Shyworm Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Shyworm]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Shyworm Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Shyworm Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Subterranean Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Subterranean Resistance ([[Attributes]]); Grants +4–40 [[Defense]] against [[Subterranean]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Shyworm Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Shyworm Shard has the following description:
- Shyworms are immensely afraid of all other living organisms, even fellow Shyworms, and tend to prefer dark places because most other critters don't.

[Item Properties]

Item ID: SKYMART_BROCHURE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Bustling reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Farming Fortune; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 9.; COMMON REFORGE STONE
The SkyMart Brochure is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Bustling]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]]. It grants [[Farming Fortune]].
## Obtaining
The SkyMart Brochure can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 100 [[Copper]].
## Usage
### Reforging
SkyMart Brochure can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Bustling]] reforge, to grant [[Farming Fortune]], which scales with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining IX (9)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Bustling]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SKYMART_VACUUM
Tooltip: Damage: +100; Farming Fortune: +5; Can be used to damage  Pests.; Ability: Vacuum HOLD RIGHT CLICK Deals 100  Damage per second to nearby  Pests.; Ability: Pest Tracker LEFT CLICK Leads you in the direction of the nearest  Pest.; Cooldown: 1s; Vacuum Bag: 0 Pests Vacuum  Pests in your Garden to increase!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Garden Level 5.; COMMON VACUUM
The SkyMart Vacuum is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Vacuums|Vacuum]].
## Obtaining
The SkyMart Vacuum can be claimed from [[Pesthunter Phillip]] in [[The Garden]] as part of his quest. It can also be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 5,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Vacuum:
SkyMart Vacuum [[Damage]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Vacuum HOLD RIGHT CLICK; Deals 100 Damage per second to nearby Pests (Pest (Mob Type)). Ability: Pest Tracker LEFT CLICK; Leads you in the direction of the nearest Pest (Pest (Mob Type)).; Cooldown: 1s]
The SkyMart Vacuum can be used to capture [[Pests]]. When used near a pest, it will deal 100 damage per second. [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]] captured with the SkyMart Vacuum will be sucked up, and can be exchanged at [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for temporary [[Farming Fortune]] and various goods at his shop.

The Slime Hat is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] early-game [[Helmets|Helmet]] unlocked from [[Slimeball|Slimeball II (2)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

Helmet:
Slime Hat [[Health]]: +50

## Trivia
- There was a bug where if you wore the Slime Hat and replaced it with a Talisman, then to a different helmet, it would continue to give you the immunity to knockback although you did not have the helmet on. You had to exclusively use shift-click for the bug to work. This has been fixed.

# Small Huntrap
Categories: Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Tender Wood Collection, Tools
[Small Huntrap]
Type: Item

Obtained: Craft
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Medium Huntrap]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 4x [[Tender Wood]] 4x [[Enchanted Fig Log]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RETIA_BASICA
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Retentio RIGHT CLICK Traps can be placed near the spawn location of a Shard creature, with time it will eventually catch it.; Fundamentum artis venandi.; COMMON TRAP
The Small Huntrap is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Huntraps|Huntrap]], whose recipe is unlocked at [[Tender Wood|Tender Wood I (1)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Tender Wood I (1) ([[Tender Wood]])|4x [[Tender Wood]] 4x [[Enchanted Fig Log]]|Recipe: [[Tender Wood]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Fig Log]] ×4 → Small Huntrap|
## Usage
A Small Huntrap can be placed near the spawn location of any creature that can drop a [[Attributes|Shard]] to collect one. The time to catch a shard is random and based on the rarity of the shard caught (see [[Huntraps|Huntraps#Time]]).
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Medium Huntrap]]|Tender Wood III (3) ([[Tender Wood]])|[[Small Huntrap]] 1x Small Huntrap 16x [[Enchanted Fig Log]] 32x [[Tender Wood]]|Recipe: [[Tender Wood]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x4 ×4 + Small Huntrap → [[Medium Huntrap]]|
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntrap's description, "Fundamentum artis vinandi." is Latin, and means "The basis of the art of hunting."
## Gallery
- A placed Huntrap

The Soul Campfire Badge is a [[Accessories|Progressive Accessory]] acquired and upgraded by completing the [[Trial of Blue Flames]]. It has a total of five tiers, going from [[Rarity|COMMON]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
## Obtaining
The Soul Campfire Badge is obtained from [[Brian]] as a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] after surviving the first level of the [[Trial of Blue Flames]]. Completing more stages of the [[Trial of Blue Flames]] raises its rarity up to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].

Speed Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Sugar Cane|Sugar Cane II (2)]]. It increases the player's [[Speed]] stat by +1. It has the appearance of a small bundle of [[Sugar Cane]], compacted into a cube.
Previously, when the player reached [[Sugar Cane|Sugar Cane V (5)]], the Speed Talisman gave 2 [[Speed]], and when the collection reached [[Sugar Cane|Sugar Cane VIII (8)]], it gave 3 [[Speed]]. This speed increase would be reflected in the stats of the Speed Talisman item in the Collection menu.

## Obtaining

Spider Hat [[Crit Chance]]: +25

## Trivia
- This item grants the most [[Crit Chance]] out of any armor piece, granting the player 45 [[Crit Chance]] in dungeon when 5-starred, recombobulated and reforged with [[Reforging|Ancient]]. However, this is not viable.
## Tips
- This is a good source of early-game [[Crit Chance]] since it's relatively easy to get by killing a few spiders, and the effect can be useful for surviving in the [[Spider's Den]], but generally not viable due to its lack of stats.

# Spiked Bait
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Fishing
[Spiked Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Pufferfish I (1) ([[Pufferfish]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 20 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPIKED_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +6 Sea Creature Chance.; COMMON BAIT
Spiked Bait is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Pufferfish I (1) ([[Pufferfish]])|1x [[Pufferfish]] 1x [[Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Raw Cod]] + [[Pufferfish]] → Spiked Bait|
### Seal's Treat Bag
128x Spiked Bait have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Spiked Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Spiked Bait will be consumed to grant +6 [[Sea Creature Chance]]. Note that the [[Sea Creature Chance]] doesn't show up in the player's stats. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Spiked Atrocity]]|None|1x [[Bait Ring]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Ink Sac]] [[Spiked Bait]] 128x Spiked Bait|Recipe: [[Enchanted Ink Sac]] x32 ×4 + Spiked Bait x32 ×4 + [[Bait Ring]] → [[Spiked Atrocity]]|

# Spray Can
Categories: Common items, Items, Craftable items, Pages with UIs, Tools, Other Tools, Cosmetics
[Spray Can]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Right-click on any wall of your choice to spray.; Left-click to access the Ink Manager and Spray Selector.
Uses: Spraying
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 5 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPRAY_CAN
Tooltip: Right-click on any wall of your choice to spray.; Left-click to access the Ink Manager and Spray Selector.; COMMON
The Spray Can is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that can only be used on a [[Private Island]] or [[Marco]]'s [[Canvas Room]]. It requires [[Vanilla Objects/Dyes|Vanilla Dyes]] to function.
## Obtaining
### Quest
Complete the [[Quests|Quest]] Marco the Artist by talking to [[Marco]] in the [[Village]].
### Crafting
After completing the [[Quests|Quest]] Marco the Artist, the Spray Can crafting recipe is unlocked (as shown below):
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Marco The Artist Quest ([[Quests]])|1x Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]) 7x [[Glass]]|Recipe: [[Glass]] ×7 + [[Redstone Dust]] → Spray Can|
## Usage
By left-clicking the Spray Can, an interface for selecting a spray and viewing the ink storage will open.
- Each Spray Can has an Ink Storage. [[Vanilla Objects/Dyes|Vanilla Dyes]] are used as spray ink. The storage can be filled with different types of ink, which is required for using sprays.
- The storage can be filled with a maximum of 100 of each of the 16 dyes, by clicking on the storage icon with a dye. One dye fills up 1%.
- If a player choose to preview a spray from the interface, the player will hold a map showing the spray image.
By right-clicking with the Spray Can on the side of a block, if the ink storage has the required ink, the player can print the selected spray on that side.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Spray Can.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Sprays are now ordered alphabetically in the Spray Can.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Added filters to the Spray Can.|

# Stone Platform
Categories: Common items, Items, Craftable items, Cobblestone Collection
[Stone Platform]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Cobblestone II (2) ([[Cobblestone]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 32 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: STONE_BRIDGE
Tooltip: Creates a stone platform in the air after travelling 10 blocks.; Perfect for building below your island!; COMMON
A Stone Platform is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item unlocked at [[Cobblestone|Cobblestone II (2)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Cobblestone II (2) ([[Cobblestone]])|1x [[Egg]] 64x [[Stone Bricks]]|Recipe: [[Stone Bricks]] x8 ×8 + [[Egg]] → Stone Platform|
## Usage
Throwing a Stone Platform creates a horizontal 8x8 platform of [[Stone Bricks]] on the [[Private Island]] after travelling 10 blocks.
If the thrown Stone Platform egg hits a block while travelling, the Stone Platform does not spawn. If it goes outside the [[Private Island]] bounds, the Stone Platform spawns at the closest area within bounds.
## Trivia
- Blocks placed by a Stone Platform cannot be destroyed with a [[Block Zapper]].
  - A [[Builder's Ruler]], however, does work.
- Despite what the tooltip says, Stone Platforms travel 6 blocks before creating a Stone Platform.
## Gallery
- A placed Stone Platform
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Stone Platform.|
