Collection ([[Collections]]): Chili Pepper IX (9) ([[Chili Pepper]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: II
Maximum Level: III
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_TABASCO
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Tabasco II Grants +2 weapon damage if you don't have a Dragon pet equipped.; Applicable on: Melee Weapon, Weapons Apply Cost: 300 Exp Levels; COMMON
Tabasco is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that grants additional damage if the player does not have a [[Ender Dragon Pet|Dragon Pet]] equipped.
## Obtaining
The Tabasco [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Tabasco II|Requirement: Chili Pepper IX (9) ([[Chili Pepper]]), Ingredients: 3x [[Kuudra Teeth]] 32x [[Chili Pepper]], [[Crafting]] recipe: Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] x16 ×2 + [[Kuudra Teeth]] ×3 → Enchanted Book|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Tabasco) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Tabasco III.
### Bazaar
The Tabasco [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Tabasco|II|
|Tabasco|III|
## Ability
Grants +2-3 weapon damage if the player does not have a Dragon Pet equipped.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|II|+2|
|III|+3|
## Applying
The Tabasco [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Weapons|Weapon]]. This includes [[Swords]], [[Fishing Rods]], [[Bows]], [[Axes]], [[Longswords]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet|Gauntlets]]. It does not require an [[Enchanting]] level to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅱ|0 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|0 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- The Dragon Pet mentioned can be either a [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon]], [[Jade Dragon Pet]], [[Rose Dragon Pet]], or [[Ender Dragon Pet]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- The enchantment's name could be a reference to tabasco sauce: a hot sauce made with vinegar, tabasco peppers and salt.
  - It could also be a reference to tabasco peppers, a variety of chili pepper.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 5, 2022|Added Tabasco II.|
|Aug 5, 2022|Added Tabasco III.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Fixed bug where combining max level enchants would sometimes lower the enchantment level.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: COIN_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Coins start appearing around you occasionally on public islands. Lucky!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
The Talisman of Coins is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that occasionally spawns [[Coins]] on the ground near the player while on public islands.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Emerald II (2) ([[Emerald]])|5x [[Gold Ingot]] 20x [[Emerald]]|Recipe: [[Emerald]] x5 ×4 + [[Gold Ingot]] x5 → Talisman of Coins|
## Usage
While held in the player's inventory or stored in the [[Accessory Bag]], the Talisman of Coins occasionally spawns a small group of [[Coins]] in the player's vicinity. Any player on the server can see and collect these coins. Picking up a group awards 5 [[Coins]] or 25 [[Coins]].
The effect does not function on the [[Private Island]] or in [[The Garden]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ring of Coins]]|None|[[Talisman of Coins]] 1x Talisman of Coins 4x [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]|Recipe: [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] ×4 + Talisman of Coins → [[Ring of Coins]]|

Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Gemstones: [] []; Allows you to harness the power of 2 Gemstones, although only half of the normal power can be harnessed!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
The Talisman of Power is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It upgrades into the [[Ring of Power]], and is unlocked at [[Gemstone|Gemstone IV (4)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Gemstone IV (4) ([[Gemstone]])|45x Flawed Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|Recipe: [[Gemstone]] x5 ×9 → Talisman of Power|
## Usage
The Talisman of Power can have 2 [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] applied to it in the [[Geo|Gemstone Grinder]] at [[Geo]]. Applied Gemstones provide half of the stats they would usually give.
The following Gemstones can be placed in the Talisman of Power:
- [[Gemstone|Ruby]]
- [[Gemstone|Amber]]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ring of Power]]|Gemstone VI (6) ([[Gemstone]])|[[Talisman of Power]] 1x Talisman of Power 1x [[Gemstone Mixture]] 7x Fine Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|Recipe: [[Gemstone]] ×7 + [[Gemstone Mixture]] + Talisman of Power → [[Ring of Power]]|
## Trivia
- Unlike the [[Artifact of Power]] and [[Relic of Power]], the Talisman of Power does not upgrade in rarity when all [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] applied to it are Perfect.

The Tank Zombie Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Undead Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Tank Zombie Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Tank Zombie]] or [[Super Tank Zombie]] with enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Tank Zombie Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||

[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Tank Zombie Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Undead Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Undead Resistance ([[Attributes]]); Grants +4–40 [[Defense]] against Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

The player can purchase a Tepid Green Tea for 1,000 [[Coins]] from the [[Bartender]].

Tepid Green Tea buffs the [[Defense]] value of [[Potions]] with that stat by 10%. It can be used in place of an [[Awkward Potion]] in a [[Brewing Stand]] to take effect.

Tree-like Wool is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Tree-like Wool can be obtained by breaking the tree found on [["Your" Island]].
## Usage
The player can use Tree-like Wool to bridge across the gap in [["Your" Island]] to access [[Jerry (Rift)|Jerry]] on the other island.
## Trivia
- The internal ID of the item, BEDWARS_WOOL is a reference to another Hypixel miningame, Bedwars, where wool is the preferred bridging block amongst many players.

[Shop]
Sell: 10 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TUNGSTEN
Tooltip: Collection Item; Considered for a long time to be unbreakable prior to the discovery of the Crystal Hollows.; COMMON DWARVEN METAL
Tungsten is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Dwarven Metals|Dwarven Metal]] in the [[Mining]] Collection.
## Collection
Collecting Tungsten increases the player's Tungsten [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of Tungsten.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|100|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|II|1,000|[[Enchanted Tungsten]] Recipe|+4|
|III|5,000|[[Refined Tungsten]] Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|IV|10,000|[[Tungsten Key]] Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|V|25,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|VI|50,000|[[Tungsten Plate]] Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|VII|100,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|VIII|250,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|IX|500,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
## Obtaining
Tungsten is obtained from mining [[Cobblestone]] or [[Clay]] blocks in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] or [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. In [[Glacite Mineshafts]], it can also take the appearance of [[Cobblestone Slab]] or [[Cobblestone Stairs]]. It requires 9 [[Breaking Power]] to mine. All types have 5,600 [[Block Strength]], but the amount of base drops varies.
|Slot|Block|Multiplier|
|-|-|-|
|[[Cobblestone]]|[[Cobblestone]]|1|
|[[Cobblestone Slab]]|[[Cobblestone Slab]]|1|
|[[Cobblestone Stairs]]|[[Cobblestone Stairs]]|1|
|[[Clay]]|[[Clay]]|3|

# Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison
Categories: Common items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Potions
[Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison]
Type: [[Brews]]
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Shifty]]
Buy: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TUTTI_FRUTTI_POISON
Tooltip: Basic Brew; Use this item in place of an Awkward Potion for certain potions.; Adds +5% to the damage of Archery potions.; COMMON
Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Brews|Brew]]. It can be purchased for 1,000 [[Coins]] from [[Shifty]].
## Usage
### Crafting
Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison can be used to craft [[Poisoned Candy]], that is used to get the [[Great Spook Armor|Great Spook Leggings]] from [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Poisoned Candy]]|[[The Great Spook]] Talking to the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]|1x [[Simple Carrot Candy]] 4x [[Enchanted Poisonous Potato]] [[Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison]] 4x Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison|Recipe: Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison ×4 + [[Enchanted Poisonous Potato]] ×4 + [[Simple Carrot Candy]] → [[Poisoned Candy]]|
### Brewing
#### Archery Potion
Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison can be used with an [[Archery Potion]] to add +5% damage to it. It can be used in place of an [[Awkward Potion]] in a [[Brewing Stand]] to take effect.
Recipe: Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison → [[Archery Potion]], [[Archery Potion]], [[Archery Potion]]
#### Poisoned Candy I Potion
Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison can be used to brew a Poisoned Candy I Potion. This potion has no known uses, but can be drank, or converted into a splash potion and splashed.
##### Input
Recipe: [[Simple Carrot Candy]] → Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison, Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison, Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison
##### Output
Recipe:  → Poisoned Candy I Potion, Poisoned Candy I Potion, Poisoned Candy I Potion
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 3, 2019|Added Tutti-Frutti Flavored Poison.|

[Shop]
Sell: 10 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: UMBER
Tooltip: Collection Item; Hardy enough to flourish even in the harshest of conditions.; COMMON DWARVEN METAL
Umber is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Dwarven Metals|Dwarven Metal]] in the [[Mining]] Collection.
## Collection
Collecting Umber increases the player's Umber [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of Umber.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|100|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|II|1,000|[[Enchanted Umber]] Recipe|+4|
|III|5,000|[[Refined Umber]] Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|IV|10,000|[[Umber Key]] Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|V|25,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|VI|50,000|[[Umber Plate]] Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|VII|100,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|VIII|250,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|IX|500,000|[[Umberella]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
Umber is obtained from mining [[Hardened Clay]], [[Hardened Clay|Brown Hardened Clay]] or [[Red Sandstone]] blocks in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] or [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. It requires 9 [[Breaking Power]] to mine. All types have 5,600 [[Block Strength]], but the amount of base drops varies.
|Slot|Block|Multiplier|
|-|-|-|
|[[Hardened Clay]]|[[Hardened Clay]]|1|
|[[Hardened Clay]]|Brown Hardened Clay ([[Hardened Clay]])|2|
|[[Red Sandstone]]|[[Red Sandstone]]|3|

Next →: [[Vaccine Ring]]

Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VACCINE_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Reduces the effect of poison by 10%.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
Vaccine Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that reduces the effect of [[Poison Potion|Poison]] by 10%.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Potato III (3) ([[Potato]])|9x [[Poisonous Potato]]|Recipe: [[Poisonous Potato]] ×9 → Vaccine Talisman|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Vaccine Ring]]|None|[[Vaccine Talisman]] 1x Vaccine Talisman 8x [[Arachne's Fang]]|Recipe: [[Arachne's Fang]] ×8 + Vaccine Talisman → [[Vaccine Ring]]|
## Notes
- Poisonous Potatoes can be obtained on [[Private Island]] when breaking full grown crops with water. Breaking the crops normally will not yield any. They are also a common RNG drop you can get by killing zombies in the graveyard.
- It is possible to obtain the materials for this fairly quickly by using a [[Zombie Minion]].
- [[Venomous Potion|Venomous]] and [[Poison Potion|Poison]] potions, when splashed by the player, will not affect the player with or without this talisman.

# Veilshroom
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Veilshroom]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VEILSHROOM
Tooltip: It hides from the light, shrouded in mystery.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; COMMON MUTATION
A Veilshroom is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Veilshroom can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 1 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]] and 1 [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]] within 1 block of [[Mycelium]].
When placed near another crop, Veilshroom increases yield by 30% and reduces watering status by -30%.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Veilshrooms also drop 28 [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], 28 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]], and +240 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Veilshroom can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 10,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 5 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Veilshroom grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Veilshrooms are used to create a [[Thornshade]] and [[Do-not-eat-shroom]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone I (1) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x [[Enchanted Leather]] 4x [[Dustgrain]] [[Veilshroom]] 4x Veilshroom|Recipe: Veilshroom ×4 + [[Dustgrain]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Leather]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
### Pet Upgrades
[[Veilshroom]] 2 Veilshroom are needed to upgrade a [[Crow Pet]] from [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] to [[Rarity|RARE]].
## Gallery
- Veilshroom in the Greenhouse Veilshroom in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Veilshroom.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Veilshroom.|
|Feb 2, 2026|Reduced Veilshroom growing requirements to 1 Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) and 1 Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]).|

The Verdant Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Forest Fishing]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Verdant Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Verdant]] with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].
### Huntraps
A Verdant Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Murkwater Depths]] (Underwater) or [[Murkwater Shallows]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Verdant Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Verdant Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Forest Fishing]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Forest Fishing ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3–30 [[Fishing Speed]] while on Foraging Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Verdant Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VISITORS_GRATITUDE
Tooltip: You made someone smile today! ; Right-click to view recipes!; COMMON
Visitors' Gratitude is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item obtained from charming [[Garden Visitors]]. It is used to craft the [[Gratitude Talisman]] and its upgrades.
## Obtaining
After [[Garden Visitors|charming]] a Garden Visitor with a [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set]], they have a 100% chance to reward a Visitors' Gratitude on their next visit.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gratitude Talisman]]|None|[[Visitors' Gratitude]] 5x Visitors' Gratitude|Recipe: Visitors' Gratitude ×5 → [[Gratitude Talisman]]|
|[[Gratitude Ring]]|None|1x [[Gratitude Talisman]] [[Visitors' Gratitude]] 8x Visitors' Gratitude|Recipe: Visitors' Gratitude ×8 + [[Gratitude Talisman]] → [[Gratitude Ring]]|
|[[Gratitude Artifact]]|None|1x [[Gratitude Ring]] [[Visitors' Gratitude]] 16x Visitors' Gratitude|Recipe: Visitors' Gratitude x2 ×8 + [[Gratitude Ring]] → [[Gratitude Artifact]]|
## Trivia
- Crafting the 29 [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set|Carrot-Colored Vinyl Sets]] required for the [[Gratitude Artifact]]'s 29 Visitors' Gratitudes costs .
  - This can be broken down into for a [[Gratitude Talisman]], for a [[Gratitude Ring]], and for a [[Gratitude Artifact]].

The Voracious Spider Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Arthropod Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Voracious Spider Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Voracious Spider]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Voracious Spider Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Spider Mound]].
### Fusion
A Voracious Spider Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Voracious Spider Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Arthropod Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Arthropod Resistance ([[Attributes]]); Grants +4–40 [[Defense]] against [[Arthropod]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Voracious Spider Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Voracious Spider Shard has the following description:
- These predatory arachnids are known for their insatiable appetite, devouring anything that crosses their path with alarming speed.

# Voter's Badge
Aliases: Voter, Voter Badge, Voters Badge, Voters badge
Categories: Common items, Accessories
[Voter's Badge]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])

[Upgrades]
← Previous: None

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)

[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VOTER_BADGE
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Grants +3 votes in Mayor Elections.; * Co-op Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
Voter's Badge is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
While a mayoral candidate is elected as [[Mayor Election|Mayor]], they have a chance to appear as a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]]. A Voter's Badge will be rewarded to the player after accepting the offer of 1 unique mayor visitor.
If lost, the Voter's Badge can be repurchased at [[Rusty]] for 20,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
The Voter's Badge grants +3 votes in [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] while in the player's [[Accessory Bag]].
## Trivia
- The Voter's Badge cannot have its rarity upgraded using a [[Recombobulator 3000]].
- Voter's Badge progress is on a per-player basis, meaning a player accepting a mayor visitor will not count towards their co-op members' progress.

The White Belt is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Belts|Belt]]. It is obtainable from [[Master Tao]] as the first [[Dojo]] reward. It gives +10 [[Health]] and +5 [[Defense]]. Its ability reduces all damage taken by 0.5%. This item is automatically Soulbound upon receiving it.
## Usage
Belt:
White Belt [[Health]]: +10 [[Defense]]: +5
[Effects]
[Reduces damage taken by 0.5%.]
## Upgrading
The White Belt can be upgraded into the [[Yellow Belt]] after reaching 1000 points in the [[Dojo]].
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Crimson Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|200 Essence; 10x [[Kada Lead]]; 20x [[Magmag]]|200 Essence; 10x [[Kada Lead]]; 20x [[Magmag]]|
|✪✪|500 Essence; 20x [[Kada Lead]]; 30x [[Magmag]]|700 Essence; 30x [[Kada Lead]]; 50x [[Magmag]]|
## Trivia
- This is the only belt from the [[Dojo]] that is received without needing to reach a specific score.
- Together with its successors, it has an uncommon limit to how many [[Star Upgrades]] can be applied to it.
  - Furthermore this item has this limit at its lowest of all starrable items in the game.
- The price of this item at [[Rusty]] is not affected by the [[Shady Ring]] and its upgraded versions.

# White Gift
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, More info needed, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Events, Season of Jerry, Gifts
[White Gift]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]

Next →: [[Green Gift]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Winter Sack]]; [[Events Sack]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WHITE_GIFT
Tooltip: Click a stranger while holding to gift!; Both players get the rewards!; COMMON
White Gift is a [[Gifts|Gift]] obtained from [[Jerry's Workshop]]. A player can give this to another player, allowing both of them to receive rewards.
## Obtaining
### Gift Locations
Every SkyBlock Year, the player can earn up to 20 White Gifts by claiming them in specific locations[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) across [[Jerry's Workshop]]. These gifts stay in the same place each year. Players can use the [[Gift Compass]] to locate them.
### Frozen Treasure
Within the [[Glacial Cave]], players can mine [[Glacial Cave|Frozen Treasure]] to obtain White Gifts.
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv25]|[[Grinch]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv3]|Frosty ([[Frosty (mob)]])|0-1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv1]|[[Frozen Steve]]|0-1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
Damaging a [[Reindrake]] also has a 65% chance to drop a White Gift.
### Season of Jerry
During the [[Season of Jerry]] event, players can earn Gifts in [[Jerry's Workshop]] of all kinds across waves for [[Defend Jerry's Workshop|defending]] the village.
### Abiphone Contact
Every SkyBlock Year, one White Gift can be obtained by calling [[St. Jerry]] with an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]].
## Usage
### Gifts
A White Gift can be given to another player to spawn a present in which that player can open, with both players receiving the item inside. Once opened, the item will drop on the ground and only the 2 players that opened and gifted the present will be able to pick up the items.
[[Snowman Mask]], [[White Gift Talisman]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] line, [[Snowfin Shard]] and the Professional Gifter perk from [[Doug]] give more rewards from gifts. White Gifts also have a 10% chance to give the player 1 [[North Stars|North Star]].

## Tips
- [[Snow Suit Armor|Snow Suit]] (including equipment) and [[Nutcracker Armor]], [[Reindeer Pet]], [[Hideongift Shard]], the Season of Joy perk from [[Essence Shops|Ice Essence Shop]] and the Present Connoisseur perk from [[Doug]] grant more Gifts from the [[Season of Jerry]] event.
## Trivia
- Sometimes, when a player opens another player's Gift, the player who placed the gift received both items. This usually occurs when a lot of gifts is placed on the same spot.
- The current Bazaar instant buy price of a White Gift is . White Gifts drop on average 2,082 [[Coins]], or 4,164 [[Coins]] when counting the rewards for both players, such as in a [[Co-op]]. They also drop on average 0.1 [[North Stars]] and other rewards, but the coin values of these rewards varies.

## Obtaining
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Wild Rose is obtained by breaking [[Mutations]] in the [[Greenhouse]] or killing [[Praying Mantis|Praying Mantises]] in [[The Garden]].
## Usage
### Mutations
Wild Roses are used to create a [[Thornshade]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Wild Rose]]|Wild Rose I (1)|[[Wild Rose]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Wild Rose|Recipe: Wild Rose x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Wild Rose]]|
## Gallery
- Wild Rose in the Greenhouse Wild Rose in the [[Greenhouse]]
### Growth Stages of Wild Rose in the Garden
- Stage 1
- Stage 2
- Stage 3
- Stage 4
## Tips
- In order to obtain the first Wild Rose to plant on an [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] or [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] profile, players must get a [[Praying Mantis]] [[Pests|Pest]], vacuum it up, and then paste the default Wild Rose preset in the [[The Garden|Desk]] using the [[Enchanted Wild Rose|Enchanted Wild Roses]]. After that, they can harvest the pasted crops to get regular Wild Roses.
  - Alternatively, players may obtain it by growing a [[Witherbloom]] in the [[Greenhouse]]. However, Greenhouse crop drops are automatically compacted, so may still be necessary to paste the default preset using the drops.
  - Another alternative is growing a [[Godseed]], however this is not recommended because it takes a really long time.

Item ID: WINTER_ISLAND
Tooltip: Place this in your private world to spawn a Winter island!; COMMON
The Winter Island is a [[Spawnable Islands|Spawnable Island]] obtainable from [[Gifts]].
## Obtaining
A Winter Island has a 0.10% (1 in 969.12 gifts) chance to be obtained from a [[Red Gift]]. Both the donating and receiving [[Player]] get the item.
## Usage
Placing Winter Island creates an Island composed of [[Stone]], [[Dirt]], [[Snow Block|Snow Blocks]], [[Packed Ice]], [[Spruce Log]], [[Leaves|Spruce Leaves]], [[Wooden Fence|Spruce Fences]], and [[Ice]].
It also contains a [[Winter Crystal]], which increases the speed of all [[Snow Minion|Snow Minions]] and [[Ice Minion|Ice Minions]] on the [[Private Island]] by 5%, and spawns a [[Gifts|Gift]] ( [[White Gift|White]], [[Green Gift|Green]] or [[Red Gift|Red]]) once every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] in a 15 block radius.
## Trivia
- This item shares the same name as the [[Jerry's Workshop|Winter Island]], where [[Jerry's Workshop]] is located.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 30, 2022|Added Winter Island.|
Linked from: [[Floating Crystals]], [[Island (disambiguation)]], [[Red Gift]], [[Spawnable Islands]], [[Winter Crystal]]

# Witherbloom
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Witherbloom]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WITHERBLOOM
Tooltip: A plant that only thrives in the presence of decay, its petals growing strong where life once was.; Only death thrives the living.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; COMMON MUTATION
A Witherbloom is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Witherbloom can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Dead Plant]] within 1 block of [[Soul Sand]].
When placed near another crop, Witherbloom spreads buffs and debuffs to adjacent crops.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Witherblooms also drop 184 (2 stacks plus 56) [[Wild Rose]] and +368 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Witherbloom can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 40,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 20 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Witherbloom grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Witherblooms are used to create a [[Cindershade]] and [[Snoozling]].
## Gallery
- Witherbloom in the Greenhouse Witherbloom in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Witherbloom.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Witherbloom.|
|Feb 2, 2026|Reduced Witherbloom growing requirements to 4 [[Dead Plant]].|

The Yog Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Yog Membrane]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Yog Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Yog]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Yog Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Yog Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Yog Membrane]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Yog Membrane ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Heat Resistance]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

A Yog Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

- The [[Zombie Artifact]], [[Zombie Ring]], and Zombie Talisman used to stack to get 30% reduced damage taken from [[Zombie|Zombies]].
